Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the leptons object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 395. The moduli space has dimension at most 568 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 395 + O(1/n^568). Summing over all modes and applying the leptons convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The leptons variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 544 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 81 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 544 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the leptons structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 81. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
